195:      if (dashFemale.includes(result)) {

199:      }

202:  }

205:  let processed = slug.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
206:  
207:  if (isFemale) {
208:    if (processed === 'nidoranm') return 'nidoranf';
209:    if (!processed.endsWith('f')) {
210:      processed += 'f'; // Showdown uses pikachuf, wobbuffetf, etc without dash
211:    }
212:  }
214:  return processed;

217:// Custom Sprite Component for perfect Battle Arena sizing and Showdown fallbacks
218:const PokemonBattleSprite = memo(({ pokemon, isBack, isShiny, isFemale, className, onClick, arenaMode = false, flip }: any) => {
219:  const [fallbackLevel, setFallbackLevel] = useState(0);
221:  useEffect(() => {
222:    setFallbackLevel(0);
223:  }, [pokemon.name, isShiny, isFemale]);
225:  const generateSrc = (level: number) => {
226:    const cleanName = getShowdownName(pokemon.name, isFemale);
227:    const effectiveLevel = level;
229:    // If the pokemon has sprites preloaded, try to use them!
230:    if (effectiveLevel === 0 && pokemon.sprites) {
231:      if (isFemale) {
232:        if (pokemon.sprites.other?.home?.front_female) {
233:          return isShiny 
234:            ? (pokemon.sprites.other.home.front_shiny_female || pokemon.sprites.other.home.front_female) 
235:            : pokemon.sprites.other.home.front_female;
236:        }
237:        if (pokemon.sprites.other?.['official-artwork']?.front_female) {
238:          const offArt = pokemon.sprites.other['official-artwork'];
239:          return isShiny ? (offArt.front_shiny_female || offArt.front_female) : offArt.front_female;
240:        }
241:        // If female is requested, but official arts lack it, we skip returning here 
242:        // to let it fall through to Showdown (Level 1) which often has the female sprite.
243:      } else {
244:        const offArt = pokemon.sprites.other?.['official-artwork'];
245:        if (offArt && (offArt.front_default || offArt.front_shiny)) {
246:          return isShiny ? (offArt.front_shiny || offArt.front_default) : offArt.front_default;
247:        }
248:      }
249:    }
250:    
251:    // Level 1: Showdown animated gif
252:    if (effectiveLevel <= 1) {
253:      const basePath = isBack ? `ani-back${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}` : `ani${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}`;
254:      return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/${basePath}/${cleanName}.gif`;
255:    }
256:    
257:    // Level 2: Showdown static 2D
258:    if (effectiveLevel === 2) {
259:      const basePath = isBack ? `gen5-back${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}` : `gen5${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}`;
260:      return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/${basePath}/${cleanName}.png`;
261:    }
263:    // Level 3: Pokemon object regular sprite
264:    if (effectiveLevel === 3 && pokemon.sprites) {
265:      if (isFemale) {
266:        return isBack 
267:          ? (isShiny ? (pokemon.sprites.back_shiny_female || pokemon.sprites.back_female || pokemon.sprites.back_default) : (pokemon.sprites.back_female || pokemon.sprites.back_default))
268:          : (isShiny ? (pokemon.sprites.front_shiny_female || pokemon.sprites.front_female || pokemon.sprites.front_default) : (pokemon.sprites.front_female || pokemon.sprites.front_default));
269:      }
270:      return isBack 
271:        ? (isShiny ? (pokemon.sprites.back_shiny || pokemon.sprites.back_default) : pokemon.sprites.back_default)
272:        : (isShiny ? (pokemon.sprites.front_shiny || pokemon.sprites.front_default) : pokemon.sprites.front_default);
273:    }
275:    // Fallback URL generation
276:    const idNum = pokemon.id || pokemon.url?.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop();
277:    const shinyPath = isShiny ? 'shiny/' : '';
278:    
279:    if (effectiveLevel === 4) {
280:      // Official artwork doesn't normally have female endpoint via direct URL structure here, just return default
281:      return `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${shinyPath}${idNum}.png`;
282:    }
283:    
284:    return isBack 
285:      ? `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/back/${shinyPath}${idNum}.png`
286:      : `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/${shinyPath}${idNum}.png`;
287:  };
289:  const currentSrc = generateSrc(fallbackLevel);
290:  const autoShouldFlip = isBack && (currentSrc.includes('official-artwork') || (!currentSrc.includes('back') && !currentSrc.includes('ani-back') && !currentSrc.includes('gen5-back')));
291:  const finalFlip = flip !== undefined ? flip : autoShouldFlip;
292:  
293:  const isMegaOrGmax = pokemon.name.includes('-mega') || pokemon.name.includes('-gmax');
294:  
295:  const scaleFactor = useMemo(() => {
296:    if (!pokemon) return 1;
297:    const h = pokemon.height || 10; // default 1.0m
298:    let baseScale = 1;
299:    if (h <= 4) baseScale = 0.72;        // Tiny scale (e.g. Joltik, Diglett)
300:    else if (h <= 10) baseScale = 0.88;   // Small-Medium scale (e.g. Pikachu, Eevee)
301:    else if (h <= 20) baseScale = 1.05;   // Normal-Large scale (e.g. Onyx, Charizard)
302:    else baseScale = 1.25;                // Huge scale (e.g. Wailord, Steelix, Groudon)
303:    
304:    if (isMegaOrGmax) {
305:      baseScale *= 1.15; // Megas and G-Max look grand and powerful
306:    }
307:    return baseScale;
308:  }, [pokemon.height, pokemon.name, isMegaOrGmax]);
310:  const [clickAura, setClickAura] = useState(false);
312:  const handleClick = (e: any) => {
313:    if (isMegaOrGmax) {
314:      setClickAura(true);
315:      setTimeout(() => setClickAura(false), 500);
316:    }
317:    if (onClick) onClick(e);
318:  };
320:  return (
321:    <div 
322:      className={cn(
323:        "relative flex items-center justify-center w-full h-full transition-all duration-700",
324:        className
325:      )}
326:      onClick={handleClick}
327:    >
328:      {clickAura && isMegaOrGmax && (
329:        <motion.div 
330:          className={cn("absolute inset-0 rounded-full blur-xl mix-blend-screen pointer-events-none -inset-4", pokemon.name.includes('-mega') ? "bg-cyan-500" : "bg-red-500")}
331:          initial={{ opacity: 1, scale: 0.8 }}
332:          animate={{ opacity: 0, scale: 1.6 }}
333:          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: "easeOut" }}
334:        />
335:      )}
336:      <motion.img
337:        src={currentSrc}
338:        alt={pokemon.name}
339:        onError={() => {
340:          if (fallbackLevel < 5) {
341:            setFallbackLevel(prev => prev + 1);
342:          }
343:        }}
344:        initial={{ scaleX: finalFlip ? -scaleFactor * 0.9 : scaleFactor * 0.9, scaleY: scaleFactor * 0.9, opacity: 0 }}
345:        animate={{ scaleX: finalFlip ? -scaleFactor : scaleFactor, scaleY: scaleFactor, opacity: 1, y: arenaMode ? [0, -10, 0] : 0 }}
346:        transition={{ 
347:          duration: 0.5, 
348:          y: { duration: 3, repeat: Infinity, ease: "easeInOut" } 
349:        }}
350:        className={cn(
351:          "object-contain w-full h-full drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(255,255,255,0.4)]"
352:        )}
353:        style={{
354:          filter: isShiny && fallbackLevel > 0 ? 'hue-rotate(60deg) saturate(1.5)' : 'none'
355:        }}
356:        referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
357:      />
358:    </div>
359:  );
360:});

417:      healing: "Heilungszellen aktiviert!"
418:    },
419:    en: {
420:      default: `${pokeName} unleashes pure power!`,
421:      fire: "Let the raging flames burn through your defenses!",
422:      water: "Get swept away by the hydro tidal currents!",
423:      electric: "Maximum high-voltage shock discharge!",
424:      grass: "The power of wild nature reclaims its hold!",
425:      ice: "Freeze under the weight of absolute zero!",
426:      psychic: "The power of the mind transcends physical force!",
427:      dragon: "The dragon's true wrath is unleashed!",
428:      boost: "My energy reserves are expanding to their absolute maximum!",
429:      protect: "Laying down impenetrable energy shields!",
430:      healing: "Cellular reconstruction process initialized!"
431:    }
432:  };
434:  const pool = translations[lang] || translations['en'];
435:  
436:  if (lowerMove.includes('protect') || lowerMove.includes('detect') || lowerMove.includes('substitute')) return pool.protect;
437:  if (lowerMove.includes('recover') || lowerMove.includes('heal') || lowerMove.includes('roost') || lowerMove.includes('rest')) return pool.healing;
438:  if (lowerMove.includes('dance') || lowerMove.includes('calm') || lowerMove.includes('nasty') || lowerMove.includes('swords') || lowerMove.includes('charge')) return pool.boost;
439:  
440:  // check category or type
441:  if (lowerMove.includes('fire') || lowerMove.includes('burn') || lowerMove.includes('flame')) return pool.fire;
442:  if (lowerMove.includes('water') || lowerMove.includes('wave') || lowerMove.includes('surf') || lowerMove.includes('hydro')) return pool.water;
443:  if (lowerMove.includes('bolt') || lowerMove.includes('thunder') || lowerMove.includes('spark') || lowerMove.includes('shock')) return pool.electric;
444:  if (lowerMove.includes('leaf') || lowerMove.includes('giga') || lowerMove.includes('seed') || lowerMove.includes('grass')) return pool.grass;
445:  if (lowerMove.includes('ice') || lowerMove.includes('freeze') || lowerMove.includes('blizzard') || lowerMove.includes('chill')) return pool.ice;
446:  if (lowerMove.includes('psych') || lowerMove.includes('mind') || lowerMove.includes('zen') || lowerMove.includes('teleport')) return pool.psychic;
447:  if (lowerMove.includes('dragon') || lowerMove.includes('draco') || lowerMove.includes('claw') || lowerMove.includes('outrage')) return pool.dragon;
448:  
449:  return pool.default;
450:};

550:    <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 w-0.5 h-0.5 border-t border-r border-current opacity-40 group-hover:opacity-100"></div>

556:const WeatherEffect = memo(({ weather }: { weather: string | null }) => {
557:  if (!weather) return null;
558:  
559:  const drops = useMemo(() => [...Array(20)].map(() => ({
560:    left: Math.random() * 100,
561:    delay: Math.random() * 2,
562:    targetLeft: (Math.random() * 100) - 5
563:  })), []);
565:  const sand = useMemo(() => [...Array(15)].map(() => ({
566:    top: Math.random() * 100,
567:    delay: Math.random() * 2
568:  })), []);
570:  return (
571:    <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none z-20 overflow-hidden">
572:      {weather === 'rain' && (
573:        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-blue-500/10">
574:          {drops.map((drop, i) => (
575:            <motion.div
576:              key={i}
577:              className="absolute w-0.5 h-8 bg-blue-400/40"
578:              style={{ left: `${drop.left}%`, top: `-10%` }}
579:              animate={{ top: '110%', left: `${drop.targetLeft}%` }}
580:              transition={{ duration: 0.6, repeat: Infinity, delay: drop.delay, ease: "linear" }}
581:            />
582:          ))}
583:        </div>
584:      )}
585:      {weather === 'sun' && (
586:        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-orange-500/5">
587:          <motion.div 
588:            className="absolute -top-20 -right-20 w-64 h-64 rounded-full"
589:            style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(251,146,60,0.2) 0%, transparent 70%)' }}
590:            animate={{ scale: [1, 1.2, 1], opacity: [0.3, 0.6, 0.3] }}
591:            transition={{ duration: 4, repeat: Infinity }}
592:          />
593:        </div>
594:      )}
595:      {weather === 'sandstorm' && (
596:        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-amber-900/10">
597:          {sand.map((s, i) => (
598:            <motion.div
599:              key={i}
600:              className="absolute w-32 h-1"
601:              style={{ left: `-20%`, top: `${s.top}%`, background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(180,83,9,0.2) 0%, transparent 70%)' }}
602:              animate={{ left: '120%' }}
603:              transition={{ duration: 1.5, repeat: Infinity, delay: s.delay, ease: "linear" }}
604:            />
605:          ))}
606:        </div>
607:      )}
608:    </div>
609:  );

612:const PokemonCardSprite = memo(({ pokemonName, id, className, isShiny }: { pokemonName: string; id: string | undefined; className: string; isShiny?: boolean }) => {
613:  const [fallbackLvl, setFallbackLvl] = useState(0);
615:  useEffect(() => {
616:    setFallbackLvl(0);
617:  }, [pokemonName, isShiny]);
619:  const getSrcAtLevel = (lvl: number): string => {
620:    const shinyPath = isShiny ? 'shiny/' : '';
621:    const cleanName = getShowdownName(pokemonName);
622:    
623:    if (lvl === 0) {
624:      return `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/other/official-artwork/${shinyPath}${id}.png`;
625:    }
626:    if (lvl === 1) {
627:      // Showdown 2D png fallback (good for megas and gmax)
628:      return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/gen5${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}/${cleanName}.png`;
629:    }
630:    if (lvl === 2) {
631:      // Showdown Animated gif fallback
632:      return `https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/ani${isShiny ? '-shiny' : ''}/${cleanName}.gif`;
633:    }
634:    // Final fallback to raw PokeAPI sprite
635:    return `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/${shinyPath}${id}.png`;
636:  };
638:  const currentSrc = getSrcAtLevel(fallbackLvl);
640:  return (
641:    <img
642:      src={currentSrc}
643:      alt={pokemonName}
644:      referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
645:      draggable={false}
646:      className={cn(className, "w-full h-full object-contain scale-[1.1] group-hover:scale-[1.3] drop-shadow-[0_10px_15px_rgba(34,211,238,0.2)]")}
647:      loading="lazy"
648:      onError={() => {
649:        if (fallbackLvl < 3) setFallbackLvl(l => l + 1);
650:      }}
651:    />
652:  );
653:});
655:const PokemonCard = memo(({ p, isSelected, isOpponentSelected, enableAnimations, onClick, isShiny }: any) => {
656:  const id = p.url.split('/').filter(Boolean).pop();
657:  const displayId = p.displayId || p.baseId || id;
658:  const isSpecial = parseInt(id || "0") > 1025 && !p.displayId;
659:  const isMega = p.name.includes('-mega');
660:  const isGmax = p.name.includes('-gmax');
661:  
662:  const [clickAura, setClickAura] = useState(false);
664:  const handleClick = () => {
665:    if (isMega || isGmax) {
666:      setClickAura(true);
667:      setTimeout(() => setClickAura(false), 500);
668:    }
669:    onClick(p.name);
670:  };
671:  
672:  return (
673:    <div
674:      key={p.name}
675:      role="button"
676:      tabIndex={0}
677:      onClick={handleClick}
678:      onKeyDown={(e) => {
679:        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
680:          e.preventDefault();
681:          handleClick();
682:        }
683:      }}
684:      onMouseEnter={() => sounds.hover()}
685:      className={cn(
686:        "border rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col items-center transition-all group cursor-pointer relative overflow-hidden h-32 sm:h-36 justify-center will-change-transform",
687:        "bg-slate-950/40 border-slate-800/50 hover:bg-cyan-950/20 hover:border-cyan-500/40 shadow-lg hover:shadow-cyan-500/10",
688:        enableAnimations && "hover:-translate-y-1 duration-300 ease-out",
689:        isSelected && "bg-cyan-900/40 border-cyan-400 shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(34,211,238,0.25)] ring-1 ring-cyan-400/50 z-10",
690:        isOpponentSelected && "bg-red-900/40 border-red-400 shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(239,68,68,0.25)] ring-1 ring-red-400/50 z-10"
691:      )}
692:    >
693:      {clickAura && (isMega || isGmax) && (
694:        <motion.div 
695:          className={cn("absolute inset-0 z-0 blur-md mix-blend-screen pointer-events-none -inset-2", isMega ? "bg-cyan-500/50" : "bg-red-500/50")}
696:          initial={{ opacity: 1, scale: 0.8 }}
697:          animate={{ opacity: 0, scale: 1.5 }}
698:          transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: "easeOut" }}
699:        />
700:      )}
701:      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/5 to-transparent opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-500"></div>
702:      <HUDCorners />
703:      
704:      {/* ID Badge */}
705:      <div className="absolute top-2 left-2.5 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 text-[7px] font-bold font-mono text-cyan-600 group-hover:text-cyan-400 group-hover:border-cyan-500/30 transition-all flex items-center gap-1 z-20">
706:        <span className={cn("w-1 h-1 rounded-full bg-cyan-900 group-hover:bg-cyan-400", enableAnimations && "group-hover:animate-pulse")}></span>
707:        {isSpecial && !isMega && !isGmax
708:          ? "SPECIAL" 
709:          : `#${String(displayId || "0").padStart(4, '0')}`}
710:      </div>
712:      {/* Scanline Effect */}
713:      <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none opacity-[0.03] group-hover:opacity-[0.07] transition-opacity z-30 bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(18,16,16,0)_50%,rgba(0,0,0,0.25)_50%),linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(255,0,0,0.06),rgba(0,255,0,0.02),rgba(0,0,255,0.06))] bg-[length:100%_2px,3px_100%]"></div>
715:      <div className="relative w-20 h-20 sm:w-24 sm:h-24 flex items-center justify-center z-10">
716:        {/* Sprite Glow */}
717:        <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-500" style={{ background: 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(6,182,212,0.05) 0%, transparent 70%)' }}></div>
718:        
719:        {enableAnimations && (isSelected || isOpponentSelected) && (
720:          <motion.div 
721:            className={cn(
722:              "absolute inset-0 rounded-full opacity-20",
723:              isSelected ? "bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(34,211,238,1)_0%,transparent_70%)]" : "bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgba(248,113,113,1)_0%,transparent_70%)]"
724:            )}
725:            animate={{ opacity: [0.1, 0.3, 0.1], scale: [1, 1.3, 1] }}
726:            transition={{ duration: 3, repeat: Infinity }}
727:          />
728:        )}
729:        <PokemonCardSprite
730:          pokemonName={p.name}
731:          id={id}
732:          isShiny={isShiny}
733:          className={cn(
734:            "transition-all duration-500 will-change-transform select-none max-w-[150%] max-h-[150%]",
735:            (isSelected || isOpponentSelected) 
736:              ? "!scale-[1.6]" 
737:              : "opacity-90 group-hover:opacity-100"
738:          )}
739:        />
740:      </div>
742:      <span className={cn(
743:        "font-hud text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider sm:text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase tracking-[0.2em] mt-2 relative z-20 transition-colors truncate w-full text-center px-2",
744:        isSelected ? "text-cyan-300" : isOpponentSelected ? "text-red-300" : "text-slate-400 group-hover:text-cyan-300"
745:      )}>
746:        {p.name.replace(/-/g, ' ')}
747:      </span>
748:    </div>
749:  );

770:const BattleLog = memo(({ log, enableAnimations, turn, isBattling }: { log: (LogEntry & { turn?: number })[]; enableAnimations: boolean; turn: string; isBattling: boolean }) => {
771:  const logRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
773:  useEffect(() => {
774:    if (logRef.current) {
775:      logRef.current.scrollTop = logRef.current.scrollHeight;
776:    }
777:  }, [log.length, isBattling, turn]);
779:  return (
780:    <div 
781:      ref={logRef} 
782:      className="bg-slate-950/80 rounded-xl border border-cyan-900/30 p-2 sm:p-3 h-28 xs:h-32 md:h-full overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar font-mono text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider space-y-1 sm:space-y-1.5 shadow-inner flex-1 min-h-[120px] md:min-h-[200px] pointer-events-auto scroll-smooth" 
783:      style={{ overflowAnchor: 'none' }}
784:    >
785:      {log.slice(-50).map((entry, i) => {
786:        const isLatest = i === log.length - 1;
787:        let colorClass = "text-slate-400 border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/40";
788:        let Icon = Info;
790:        if (entry.type === 'player') { 
791:          colorClass = "text-cyan-400 border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-950/40 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(34,211,238,0.1)]"; 
792:          Icon = Swords; 
793:        }
794:        else if (entry.type === 'opponent') { 
795:          colorClass = "text-red-400 border-red-500/30 bg-red-950/40 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(248,113,113,0.1)]"; 
796:          Icon = Swords; 
797:        }
798:        else if (entry.type === 'critical') { 
799:          colorClass = "text-yellow-400 border-yellow-500/50 bg-yellow-950/40 font-bold italic"; 
800:          Icon = Zap; 
801:        }
802:        else if (entry.type === 'effective') { 
803:          colorClass = "text-green-400 border-green-500/30 bg-green-950/40"; 
804:          Icon = Target; 
805:        }
806:        else if (entry.type === 'not-effective') { 
807:          colorClass = "text-slate-400 border-slate-500/30 bg-slate-900/60"; 
808:          Icon = Shield; 
809:        }
810:        else if (entry.type === 'stat-boost') { 
811:          colorClass = "text-blue-400 border-blue-500/30 bg-blue-950/40"; 
812:          Icon = TrendingUp; 
813:        }
814:        else if (entry.type === 'stat-lower') { 
815:          colorClass = "text-purple-400 border-purple-500/30 bg-purple-950/40"; 
816:          Icon = TrendingDown; 
817:        }
818:        else if (entry.type === 'status-effect') { 
819:          colorClass = "text-orange-400 border-orange-500/30 bg-orange-950/40"; 
820:          Icon = AlertTriangle; 
821:        }
822:        else if (entry.type === 'faint') { 
823:          colorClass = "text-red-500 border-red-600/50 bg-red-950/60 font-bold uppercase tracking-tighter"; 
824:          Icon = Skull; 
825:        }
826:        else if (entry.type === 'system') { 
827:          colorClass = "text-white border-white/20 bg-white/5 font-bold"; 
828:          Icon = Activity; 
829:        }
830:        else if (entry.type === 'normal') {
831:          if (entry.text.includes('Damage:')) { 
832:            colorClass = "text-slate-400 border-slate-700/30 italic bg-slate-900/20"; 
833:            Icon = Crosshair; 
834:          }
835:          else if (entry.text.includes('DEFENSE') || entry.text.includes('PROTECT')) {
836:            Icon = Shield;
837:          }
838:        }
840:        return (
841:          <div 
842:            key={i} 
843:            className={cn(
844:              "border rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 transition-all duration-300",
845:              enableAnimations && !isLatest && "animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left-1",
846:              isLatest && "ring-1 ring-white/20 scale-[1.01] bg-white/5 shadow-lg animate-pulse",
847:              colorClass
848:            )}
849:          >
850:            {entry.turn && (
851:              <span className="text-[7px] opacity-40 font-mono mr-1">T{entry.turn}</span>
852:            )}
853:            <div className="flex-shrink-0 p-1 rounded bg-black/30 border border-white/5">
854:              <Icon className="w-3 h-3 sm:w-3.5 sm:h-3.5" />
855:            </div>
856:            <span className="flex-1 leading-tight">{entry.text}</span>
857:          </div>
858:        );
859:      })}
860:      {isBattling && turn === 'opponent' && (
861:        <div className="text-red-500 font-hud animate-pulse py-1.5 px-2 tracking-widest flex items-center gap-2 text-[8px] sm:text-[10px]">
862:          <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> Opponent is thinking...
863:        </div>
864:      )}
865:    </div>
866:  );

869:const HPBar = memo(({ current, max, enableAnimations }: { current: number; max: number; enableAnimations: boolean }) => {
870:  const percent = current / max;
871:  const color = percent > 0.5 ? "#4ade80" : percent > 0.2 ? "#facc15" : "#f87171";
872:  
873:  return (
874:    <div className="relative h-1 sm:h-2 bg-slate-800 rounded-full border border-white/10 overflow-hidden mb-1 mt-1">

1052:  const getBattleStrategy = useCallback(async () => {
1053:    if (!pokemon || !battleOpponent) return;
1054:    setIsAiSuggesting(true);
1055:    
1056:    try {
1057:      const battleKey = `${pokemon.name}-${battleOpponent.name}-${pokemonHP}-${opponentHP}-${pokemonStatus}-${opponentStatus}`;
1058:      if (aiCache.current[battleKey]) {
1059:        setBattleSuggestion(aiCache.current[battleKey]);
1060:        setIsAiSuggesting(false);
1061:        return;
1062:      }
1064:      const battleData = {
1065:        player: { 
1066:          name: pokemon.name, 
1067:          hpPercent: Math.round((pokemonHP / pokemonMaxHP) * 100),
1068:          status: pokemonStatus,
1069:          moves: selectedMoves.map(m => ({ name: m.name, type: m.type, power: m.power }))
1070:        },
1071:        opponent: { 
1072:          name: battleOpponent.name, 
1073:          hpPercent: Math.round((opponentHP / opponentMaxHP) * 100),
1074:          status: opponentStatus,
1075:          types: battleOpponent.types.map(t => t.type.name)
1076:        }
1077:      };
1079:      const response = await fetch("/api/strategy", {
1080:        method: "POST",
1081:        headers: { 
1082:          "Content-Type": "application/json",
1083:          "Accept-Language": navigator.language
1084:        },
1085:        body: JSON.stringify({ 
1086:          battleData,
1087:          lang: selectedLang === 'auto' ? navigator.language : selectedLang
1088:        }),
1089:      });
1091:      const data = await response.json();
1092:      if (response.status === 429) {
1093:        setQuotaLimitReached(true);
1094:        setLastQuotaError(data.error);
1095:        setTimeout(() => setQuotaLimitReached(false), 60000);
1096:        if (data.strategy) {
1097:          aiCache.current[battleKey] = data.strategy;
1098:          setBattleSuggestion(data.strategy);
1099:          return;
1100:        }
1101:        throw new Error("QUOTA_LIMIT");
1102:      }
1103:      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || "Strategy module error");
1104:      
1105:      aiCache.current[battleKey] = data.strategy;
1106:      setBattleSuggestion(data.strategy);
1107:    } catch (err: any) {
1108:      if (err.message !== "QUOTA_LIMIT") {
1109:        console.error(err);
1110:      }
1111:      if (err.message === "QUOTA_LIMIT") {
1112:        setBattleSuggestion("System high volume. Fallback: Protect your HP and exploit type weaknesses.");
1113:      } else {
1114:        setBattleSuggestion("Focus on type advantages and HP management.");
1115:      }
1116:    } finally {
1117:      setIsAiSuggesting(false);
1118:    }
1119:  }, [pokemon, battleOpponent, pokemonHP, pokemonMaxHP, selectedMoves, opponentHP, opponentMaxHP, opponentStatus]);
1121:  const getEffectiveStat = useCallback((p: Pokemon, statName: string, isPlayer: boolean) => {
1122:    const base = p.stats.find(s => s.stat.name === statName)?.base_stat || 50;
1123:    const boost = p.name === pokemon?.name ? (customStats[statName] || 0) : 0;
1124:    
1125:    // Stat stages
1126:    const stages = isPlayer ? playerStatStages : opponentStatStages;
1127:    const stage = stages[statName] || 0;
1128:    const stageMultiplier = stage >= 0 ? (2 + stage) / 2 : 2 / (2 - stage);
1129:    
1130:    let multiplier = stageMultiplier;
1131:    
1132:    // Status effects
1133:    const status = isPlayer ? pokemonStatus : opponentStatus;
1134:    
1135:    if (status === 'PAR' && statName === 'speed') multiplier *= 0.5;
1136:    if (status === 'BRN' && statName === 'attack') multiplier *= 0.5;
1137:    
1138:    return Math.floor((base + boost) * multiplier);
1139:  }, [pokemon, customStats, playerStatStages, opponentStatStages, pokemonStatus, opponentStatus]);

1226:  const delay = useCallback((ms: number) => new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms)), []);
1228:  const executeMove = useCallback(async (attacker: Pokemon, defender: Pokemon, move: Move, isPlayer: boolean) => {
1229:    if (isAnimating) return 0;
1230:    setIsAnimating(true);
1231:    const log = (msg: string, type: LogEntry['type'] = 'normal') => setBattleLog(prev => [...prev, { text: msg, type, turn: turnNumber }].slice(-50));
1232:    const battleDelay = (ms: number) => delay(enableAnimations ? ms : Math.max(10, ms / 10));
1233:    
1234:    const attackerStatus = isPlayer ? pokemonStatus : opponentStatus;
1235:    
1236:    // Check for flinch
1237:    if (isPlayer && pokemonFlinched) {
1238:      setPokemonFlinched(false);
1239:      log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} flinched and couldn't move!`, 'status-effect');
1240:      setBattleMessage({ text: "FLINCHED!", type: 'status' });
1241:      addFloatingText("FLINCHED!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1242:      await battleDelay(800);
1243:      setBattleMessage(null);
1244:      return 0;
1245:    } else if (!isPlayer && opponentFlinched) {
1246:      setOpponentFlinched(false);
1247:      log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} flinched and couldn't move!`, 'status-effect');
1248:      setBattleMessage({ text: "FLINCHED!", type: 'status' });
1249:      addFloatingText("FLINCHED!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1250:      await battleDelay(800);
1251:      setBattleMessage(null);
1252:      return 0;
1253:    }
1255:    // Check for status effects that prevent moving
1256:    if (attackerStatus === 'PAR' && Math.random() < 0.25) {
1257:      log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} is paralyzed! It can't move!`, 'status-effect');
1258:      setBattleMessage({ text: "PARALYZED", type: 'status' });
1259:      addFloatingText("PARALYZED", 'status', !isPlayer);
1260:      sounds.statusParalysis();
1261:      await battleDelay(800);
1262:      setBattleMessage(null);
1263:      return 0;
1264:    }
1265:    if (attackerStatus === 'FRZ') {
1266:      if (Math.random() < 0.2) {
1267:        log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} thawed out!`, 'status-effect');
1268:        setBattleMessage({ text: "THAWED!", type: 'status' });
1269:        addFloatingText("THAWED!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1270:        if (isPlayer) setPokemonStatus(null); else setOpponentStatus(null);
1271:        await battleDelay(600);
1272:        setBattleMessage(null);
1273:      } else {
1274:        log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} is frozen solid!`, 'status-effect');
1275:        setBattleMessage({ text: "FROZEN!", type: 'status' });
1276:        addFloatingText("FROZEN!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1277:        sounds.statusFreeze();
1278:        await battleDelay(800);
1279:        setBattleMessage(null);
1280:        return 0;
1281:      }
1282:    }
1283:    if (attackerStatus === 'SLP') {
1284:      log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} is fast asleep...`, 'status-effect');
1285:      setBattleMessage({ text: "ASLEEP", type: 'status' });
1286:      addFloatingText("ASLEEP", 'status', !isPlayer);
1287:      sounds.statusSleep();
1288:      await battleDelay(800);
1289:      setBattleMessage(null);
1290:      return 0;
1291:    }
1293:    if (attackerStatus === 'CON') {
1294:      if (Math.random() < 0.25) {
1295:        log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} snapped out of its confusion!`, 'status-effect');
1296:        setBattleMessage({ text: "SNAPPED OUT!", type: 'status' });
1297:        addFloatingText("SNAPPED OUT!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1298:        if (isPlayer) setPokemonStatus(null); else setOpponentStatus(null);
1299:        await battleDelay(600);
1300:        setBattleMessage(null);
1301:      } else {
1302:        log(`${attacker.name.toUpperCase()} is confused!`, 'status-effect');
1303:        if (Math.random() < 0.33) {
1304:          log(`It hurt itself in its confusion!`, 'status-effect');
1305:          setBattleMessage({ text: "HURT SELF!", type: 'status' });
1306:          addFloatingText("HURT SELF!", 'status', !isPlayer);
1307:          sounds.hit();
1308:          
1309:          const level = 50;
1310:          const a = getEffectiveStat(attacker, 'attack', isPlayer);
1311:          const d = getEffectiveStat(attacker, 'defense', isPlayer);
1312:          const confusionDamage = Math.max(1, Math.floor((((2 * level / 5 + 2) * 40 * (a / d)) / 50) + 2));
1313:          
1314:          if (isPlayer) {
1315:            setPokemonHP(prev => Math.max(0, prev - confusionDamage));
1316:            setAttackerAnimation('hit');
1317:            await battleDelay(400);
1318:            setAttackerAnimation('none');
1319:          } else {
1320:            setOpponentHP(prev => Math.max(0, prev - confusionDamage));
1321:            setDefenderAnimation('hit');
1322:            await battleDelay(400);
1323:            setDefenderAnimation('none');
1324:          }
1325:          
1326:          await battleDelay(600);
1327:          setBattleMessage(null);
1328:          return 0;
1329:        }
1330:      }
1331:    }
1333:    // Accuracy check
1334:    if (move.accuracy && Math.random() * 100 > move.accuracy) {
1335:      log(`${move.name.toUpperCase()}!`, isPlayer ? 'player' : 'opponent');
1336:      log(`But it missed!`, 'normal');
1337:      setBattleMessage({ text: "MISSED!", type: 'status' });
1338:      addFloatingText("MISSED!", 'status', isPlayer);
1339:      sounds.notVeryEffective();
1340:      await battleDelay(800);
1341:      setBattleMessage(null);
1342:      return 0;

1787:  const getAiBattleSuggestion = async () => {
1788:    if (!pokemon || !battleOpponent || isAiAnalyzing) return;
1789:    
1790:    setIsAiAnalyzing(true);
1791:    setAiSuggestion(null);
1792:    sounds.scan();
1794:    setTimeout(() => {
1795:      let analysis = `**Matchup Analysis**: **${pokemon.name.toUpperCase()}** vs **${battleOpponent.name.toUpperCase()}**\n\n`;
1796:      
1797:      const weaknesses = battleOpponent.types.map(t => t.type.name);
1798:      const enemyVulnerability: string[] = [];
1799:      Object.keys(TYPE_CHART).forEach(type => {
1800:         if (getTypeEffectiveness(type, weaknesses) > 1) {
1801:            enemyVulnerability.push(type);
1802:         }
1803:      });
1805:      if (enemyVulnerability.length > 0) {
1806:        analysis += `* The enemy is weak to: **${enemyVulnerability.join(', ')}**.\n`;
1807:      } else {
1808:        analysis += `* The enemy has no major weaknesses.\n`;
1809:      }
1811:      if (selectedMoves.length > 0) {
1812:        let bestMove = null;
1813:        let highestMultiplier = -1;
1815:        selectedMoves.forEach(m => {
1816:           const mult = getTypeEffectiveness(m.type, weaknesses);
1817:           if (mult > highestMultiplier && m.power) {
1818:             highestMultiplier = mult;
1819:             bestMove = m;
1820:           }
1821:        });
1823:        if (highestMultiplier > 1 && bestMove) {
1824:           analysis += `* **Recommendation**: Use **${bestMove.name.toUpperCase()}** - It's super effective!\n`;
1825:        } else if (highestMultiplier < 1) {
1826:           analysis += `* **Warning**: Your attacks are not very effective. Consider swapping or using status moves.\n`;
1827:        } else if (bestMove) {
1828:           analysis += `* **Recommendation**: **${bestMove.name.toUpperCase()}** is your best damage option.\n`;
1829:        }
1830:      } else {
1831:        analysis += `* Please select some moves to get attack recommendations.\n`;
1832:      }
1834:      if (pokemonHP < pokemonMaxHP * 0.3) {
1835:         analysis += `* **CRITICAL**: Your HP is very low! Focus on survival!\n`;
1836:      }
1838:      setAiSuggestion(analysis);
1839:      sounds.success();
1840:      setIsAiAnalyzing(false);
1841:    }, 800);
1842:  };
1844:  const handleRun = () => {
1845:    sounds.flee();
1846:    setBattleLog(prev => [{ text: "PLAYER FLED THE BATTLE!", type: 'system' }, ...prev]);
1847:    setTimeout(() => {
1848:      resetSimulation();
1849:      setBattleState('setup');
1850:      setBattleLog([]);

1919:  const applyEndOfTurnEffects = async (isPlayer: boolean) => {

1934:      damage = Math.max(1, Math.floor(maxHP / 8));
1935:      log(`${isPlayer ? pokemon?.name.toUpperCase() : battleOpponent?.name.toUpperCase()} is hurt by poison!`, 'status-effect');

1937:      else setOpponentHP(prev => Math.max(0, prev - damage));
1938:      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
1939:    }

1942:  };
1944:  useEffect(() => {

1997:            );
1998:

2020:  const handlePlayerMove = async (move: Move) => {
2021:    if (turn !== 'player' || !isBattling || isAnimating || !pokemon || !battleOpponent) return;
2022:    
2023:    try {
2024:      // Deduct PP
2025:      setSelectedMoves(prev => prev.map(m => m.name === move.name ? { ...m, currentPP: Math.max(0, (m.currentPP ?? m.pp) - 1) } : m));
2026:

2028:      await applyEndOfTurnEffects(true);
2029:      
2030:      setTurn('opponent');
2031:    } catch (err) {
2032:      console.error("Error during player move:", err);
2033:    } finally {
2034:      setIsAnimating(false);
2035:    }
2036:  };
2038:  const opponentTurnStartedRef = useRef(false);
2040:  useEffect(() => {
2041:    if (battleState === 'battling' && turn === 'opponent' && opponentHP > 0 && pokemonHP > 0) {
2042:      if (opponentTurnStartedRef.current) return;
2043:      opponentTurnStartedRef.current = true;
2044:      
2045:      const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
2046:        if (!pokemon || !battleOpponent) {
2047:          opponentTurnStartedRef.current = false;
2048:          return;
2049:        }
2051:        // Strategic Move Selection AI
2052:        const chooseOptimalMove = () => {
2053:          if (!opponentMoves || opponentMoves.length === 0) return battleOpponent.moves[0];
2055:          // Filter moves with PP
2056:          const availableMoves = opponentMoves.filter(m => (m.currentPP ?? m.pp) > 0);
2057:          
2058:          if (availableMoves.length === 0) {
2059:            return opponentMoves[0];
2060:          }
2062:          let bestMove = availableMoves[0];
2063:          let highestScore = -1;
2064:          const defenderTypes = pokemon.types.map(t => t.type.name);
2065:          const opponentHealthPercent = (opponentHP / opponentMaxHP) * 100;
2066:          const playerHealthPercent = (pokemonHP / pokemonMaxHP) * 100;
2068:          // Get stats for better decision making
2069:          const oppAtk = battleOpponent.stats.find(s => s.stat.name === 'attack')?.base_stat || 50;
2070:          const oppSpA = battleOpponent.stats.find(s => s.stat.name === 'special-attack')?.base_stat || 50;
2071:          const defDef = pokemon.stats.find(s => s.stat.name === 'defense')?.base_stat || 50;
2072:          const defSpD = pokemon.stats.find(s => s.stat.name === 'special-defense')?.base_stat || 50;
2074:          for (const move of availableMoves) {
2075:            let score = 0;
2077:            if (move.name.toLowerCase() === 'transform') {
2078:              score += 100;
2079:            }
2081:            if (move.power && move.power > 0) {
2082:              const effectiveness = getTypeEffectiveness(move.type, defenderTypes);
2083:              let expectedDamage = move.power * effectiveness;
2084:              
2085:              if (move.damage_class === 'physical') {
2086:                expectedDamage *= (oppAtk / defDef);
2087:              } else if (move.damage_class === 'special') {
2088:                expectedDamage *= (oppSpA / defSpD);
2089:              }
2090:              
2091:              const accuracy = move.accuracy || 100;
2092:              expectedDamage *= (accuracy / 100);
2094:              score += expectedDamage;
2095:              
2096:              if (battleOpponent.types.some(t => t.type.name === move.type)) {
2097:                score *= 1.5;
2098:              }
2100:              if (playerHealthPercent < 20 && (move.priority || 0) > 0) {
2101:                score += 200;
2102:              }
2103:            }
2105:            if (move.meta?.ailment && move.meta.ailment.name !== 'none') {
2106:              if (pokemonStatus) {
2107:                score -= 50; 
2108:              } else {
2109:                const chance = move.meta.ailment_chance || 100;
2110:                score += chance * 0.5;
2111:              }
2112:            }
2114:            if (move.stat_changes && move.stat_changes.length > 0) {
2115:              score += 20;
2116:            }
2118:            if (move.meta?.healing && move.meta.healing > 0) {
2119:              if (opponentHealthPercent < 50) {
2120:                score += move.meta.healing * 2;
2121:              } else if (opponentHealthPercent > 90) {
2122:                score -= 100;
2123:              }
2124:            }
2126:            if (opponentHealthPercent < 30) {
2127:              if (move.power && move.power > 60) score += 50;
2128:            }
2130:            score += Math.random() * 15;
2132:            if (score > highestScore) {
2133:              highestScore = score;
2134:              bestMove = move;
2135:            }
2136:          }
2137:          return bestMove;
2138:        };
2140:        const move = chooseOptimalMove();
2141:        
2142:        if (move) {
2143:          try {
2144:            setOpponentMoves(prev => prev.map(m => m.name === move.name ? { ...m, currentPP: Math.max(0, (m.currentPP ?? m.pp) - 1) } : m));
2145:            
2146:            const damage = await executeMove(battleOpponent, pokemon, move, false);
2147:            
2148:            // We must use the latest state values here, or just let the state updates happen
2149:            // The issue is `pokemonHP` and `opponentHP` in this closure are stale.
2150:            // We can just rely on the state updates in `executeMove` and `applyEndOfTurnEffects`.
2151:            // Wait, `executeMove` updates the state, but `damage` is returned.
2152:            // We don't need to manually calculate `newPokemonHP` because `executeMove` already called `setPokemonHP`.
2153:            // We just need to check if the battle is over.
2154:            // Let's just set turn to player and let the battle over effect handle it.
2155:            
2156:            await applyEndOfTurnEffects(false);
2157:            
2158:            setTurnNumber(prev => prev + 1);
2159:            setTurn('player');
2160:          } catch (err) {
2161:            console.error("Error during opponent move:", err);
2162:          } finally {
2163:            setIsAnimating(false);
2164:            opponentTurnStartedRef.current = false;
2165:          }
2166:        } else {
2167:          opponentTurnStartedRef.current = false;
2168:        }
2169:      }, 500);
2170:      return () => {
2171:        clearTimeout(timer);
2172:        // We don't reset opponentTurnStartedRef here because the timer might have already fired,
2173:        // and we want to prevent concurrent executions.
2174:      };
2175:    } else {
2176:      opponentTurnStartedRef.current = false;
2177:    }
2178:  }, [turn, battleState]);
